Crux Risk Management Private Limited

Operating Structurewho owns what, and what clears you to the next level

Every chair states one thing it owns, what it must escalate, who feeds it information, and the five clearance levels between joining it and being ready for the next one.

Processes

Regions

01

Overview

Every chair on one page, by function across and by level down. Tap any chair to open its file.

Read across a lane to see one function top to bottom. Read down a column to see everyone at the same level across the company. Hatched cells mean that function has nobody at that level, which is usually deliberate. Dashed chips are open or uncounted.

The seven handoffs that hold it together
Branch collection clears local certificationhands toCentral collections pursues release
Franchise partner submits the monthly claimverified byAccounts computes entitlement independently
Accounts reconciles the varianceapproved byFinance Head releases payment
Operations creates operational datavalidated byFinance and Assurance
MIS publishes the validated numbersevaluated byMD and the Board
Business Excellence authors the standardaudited byAssurance, never itself
Any new process, system or structural changegated byBusiness Excellence before go-live

Reporting lines, function by function. Tap a block to open it, then any chair to jump to its file.

Reports to the Operations Head so that Regional and Branch Managers answer to one function for both delivery and collection. Finance retains credit limits, the AR ledger, receipt allocation and independent DSO measurement, so Operations cannot mark its own collection homework. Branch collection executives report to their Branch Manager on a solid line and to this chair on a dotted line for target, method and reporting. Dual chair with Bids and Tenders: bid pricing goes to the MD, and the Finance Head signs any credit decision for a client whose bid she prepared.

06

Growth gates

Each gate unlocks the next set of chairs. Hiring is tied to revenue run-rate, not ambition.

Gates are read on a three-month rolling annualised run-rate, not full-year actuals. Unlock as soon as the run-rate crosses the gate. Do not hire ahead of it.

One deliberate departure. The reference plan puts Quality and Compliance under the National Head of Risk Operations. This design does not, because that is the self-assurance defect the structure exists to remove. The gate is adopted; the reporting line is not.
